class="paragraph"> 人参生长在上党山谷以及辽东地区。春秋两季采挖根部，用竹刀刮去外皮，晒干。根部形状像人形的最为神奇珍贵。 </div> <div class="paragraph"> 《名医别录》记载：<span class="key-term">人参</span>生长在上党郡，位于冀州西南方向，如今魏国辽东以及高丽地区也有出产。根如人形的品质好，完全像人形的最为上品<a class="page-btn">▶</button> </div> </div> <script> // ========== STATE ========== let currentCategory = '全部'; let activeConfidences = new Set(['高', '中']); let searchQuery = ''; let sortMode = '默认排序'; const ITEMS_PER_PAGE = 6; let currentPage = 1; // ========== RENDER HERB GRID ========== function getFilteredHerbs() { let herbs = [...HERB_DATA]; // Category filter if (currentCategory !== '全部') { herbs = herbs.filter(h => h.category === currentCategory); } // Confidence filter herbs = herbs.filter(h => { const conf = h.confidence >= 4 ? '高' : h.confidence >= 3 ? '中' : '低'; return activeConfidences.has(conf); }); // Search filter if (searchQuery) { const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase(); herbs = herbs.filter(h => h.name.includes(q) || h.latin.toLowerCase().includes(q) || h.ancientNames.includes(q) ); } // Sort if (sortMode === '置信度从高到低') { herbs.sort((a, b) => b.confidence - a.confidence); } else if (sortMode === '名称 A-Z') { herbs.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name, 'zh')); } else if (sortMode === '考证完成度') { herbs.sort((a, b) => b.wordCount - a.wordCount); } return herbs; } function renderGrid() { const herbs = getFilteredHerbs(); const grid = document.querySelector('.herb-grid'); const countEl = document.querySelector('.grid-count span'); countEl.textContent = herbs.length; const start = (currentPage - 1) * ITEMS_PER_PAGE; const pageHerbs = herbs.slice(start, start + ITEMS_PER_PAGE); grid.innerHTML = pageHerbs.map(h => { const conf = h.confidence >= 4 ? '高' : h.confidence >= 3 ? '中' : '低'; const stars = '★'.repeat(h.confidence) + '☆'.repeat(5 - h.confidence); const confIcon = h.conclusionType === 'match' ? '✓' : h.conclusionType === 'migrate' ? '~' : '?'; const confClass = h.conclusionType === 'match' ? 'match' : h.conclusionType === 'migrate' ? 'migrate' : 'doubt'; return ` <div class="herb-card" onclick="location.href='duizhao-detail.html?herb=${h.id}'"> <div class="card-images"> <div class="card-img ancient"> <div class="card-img-placeholder">古图</div> <span class="img-label ancient">《图经》原图</span> </div> <div class="card-img modern"> <div class="card-img-placeholder">今照</div> <span class="img-label modern">现代实拍</span> </div> <div class="vs-badge">VS</div> </div> <div class="card-content"> <div class="card-header"> <div class="card-titles"> <h3 class="card-name">${h.name}</h3> <div class="card-latin">${h.latin}</div> </div> <span class="card-category">${h.category}</span> </div> <div class="card-conclusion"> <span class="conclusion-icon ${confClass}">${confIcon}</span> <span class="conclusion-text">${h.conclusionText}</span> </div> <div class="card-meta"> <div class="meta-item"> <span>考证置信度</span> <span class="confidence-stars">${stars}</span> </div> <span class="meta-item">${h.wordCount} 字</span> </div> </div> </div>`; }).join(''); renderPagination(herbs.length); } function renderPagination(total) { const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / ITEMS_PER_PAGE)); if (currentPage > totalPages) currentPage = totalPages; const pagination = document.querySelector('.pagination'); let html = `<button class="page-btn" ${currentPage === 1 ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick="goToPage(${currentPage - 1})">◀</button>`; for (let i = 1; i <= totalPages; i++) { if (totalPages > 7 && i > 3 && i < totalPages - 1 && Math.abs(i - currentPage) > 1) { if (i === 4) html += '<span class="page-ellipsis">...</span>'; continue; } html += `<button class="page-btn ${i === currentPage ? 'active' : ''}" onclick="goToPage(${i})">${i}</button>`; } html += `<button class="page-btn" ${currentPage === totalPages ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick="goToPage(${currentPage + 1})">▶</button>`; pagination.innerHTML = html; } function goToPage(page) { const totalPages = Math.ceil(getFilteredHerbs().length / ITEMS_PER_PAGE); if (page < 1 || page > totalPages) return; currentPage = page; renderGrid(); window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' }); } // ========== FILTER BUTTONS (real logic) ========== document.querySelectorAll('.filter-btn').forEach(btn => { btn.addEventListener('click', function() { this.parentElement.querySelectorAll('.filter-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active')); this.classList.add('active'); currentCategory = this.textContent.trim(); currentPage = 1; renderGrid(); }); }); // ========== CONFIDENCE BUTTONS (real logic) ========== document.querySelectorAll('.conf-btn').forEach(btn => { btn.addEventListener('click', function() { this.classList.toggle('active'); const confMap = { '高': '高', '中': '中', '低': '低' }; const label = this.textContent.trim(); if (activeConfidences.has(label)) { activeConfidences.delete(label); } else { activeConfidences.add(label); } currentPage = 1; renderGrid(); }); }); // ========== SEARCH (P0) ========== const searchInput = document.querySelector('.search-input'); let searchTimeout; searchInput.addEventListener('input', function() { clearTimeout(searchTimeout); searchTimeout = setTimeout(() => { searchQuery = this.value.trim(); currentPage = 1; renderGrid(); }, 300); }); // ========== SORT ========== document.querySelector('.sort-select').addEventListener('change', function() { sortMode = this.value; currentPage = 1; renderGrid(); }); // ========== INIT ========== renderGrid(); // ========== USER AUTH ========== function checkLoginStatus() { const isLoggedIn = localStorage.getItem('isLoggedIn'); const loginBtn = document.getElementById('loginBtn'); const userAvatar = document.getElementById('userAvatar'); const navUserAvatar = document.getElementById('navUserAvatar'); if (isLoggedIn) { const userData = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('benCaoUser') || '{}'); if (userData.avatar && navUserAvatar) navUserAvatar.src = userData.avatar; if (loginBtn) loginBtn.style.display = 'none'; if (userAvatar) userAvatar.style.display = 'block'; } else { if (loginBtn) loginBtn.style.display = 'block'; if (userAvatar) userAvatar.style.display = 'none'; } } function goToLogin() { window.parent.showPage('login'); } function goToProfile() { window.parent.showPage('profile'); } function goToSettings() { showToast('账号设置功能开发中...', 'info'); } function logout() { if (confirm('确定要退出登录吗？')) { localStorage.removeItem('isLoggedIn'); localStorage.removeItem('benCaoUser'); location.reload(); } } function toggleUserDropdown() { document.getElementById('userDropdown').classList.toggle('show'); } document.addEventListener('click', (e) => { const userMenu = document.getElementById('userMenu'); if (userMenu && !userMenu.contains(e.target)) { const dropdown = document.getElementById('userDropdown'); if (dropdown) dropdown.classList.remove('show'); } }); checkLoginStatus(); </script> </body> <p>画面采用工笔重彩技法，先用淡墨勾勒轮廓，再以矿物颜料层层渲染。叶片采用没骨画法，花蕊以金粉点缀，显示出宋代院体画的典型风格。</p> <p>画面右下角钤有"御览"朱文方印，表明此图曾入藏宋代内府，为皇家医药图籍。</p> </div> </div> <div class="modal-section"> <div class="modal-section-title">佐证药物</div> filterHerb = '全部药物'; let filterRegion = '全部地区'; let currentModalImages = []; let currentModalImageIndex = 0; let currentArtifact = null; let extraCardsLoaded = 0; // ========== CATEGORY TAB FILTER (P0 { const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.artifact-card'); cards.forEach(card => { const cardCategory = card.dataset.category || getCardCategory(card); const cardDynasty = card.dataset.dynasty || getCardDynasty(card); const cardHerbs = card.dataset.herbs || getCardHerbs(card); const cardRegion = card.dataset.region || getCardRegion(card); let visible = true; // Category filter if (currentCategory !== 'all' && cardCategory !== currentCategory) visible = false; // Dynasty filter if (filterDynasty !== '全部' && !cardDynasty.includes(filterDynasty)) visible = false; // Herb filter if (filterHerb !== '全部药物' && !cardHerbs.includes(filterHerb)) visible = false; // Region filter if (filterRegion !== '全部地区' && !cardRegion.includes(filterRegion)) visible = false; card.style.display = visible ? '' : 'none'; }); } function getCardCategory(card) { const typeEl = card.querySelector('.artifact-type'); if (!typeEl) return 'all'; const text = typeEl.textContent.trim(); if (text.includes('药图')) return 'painting'; if (text.includes('宋画')) return 'art'; if (text.includes('出土')) return 'excavation'; if (text.includes('药具')) return 'utensil'; return 'all'; } function getCardDynasty(card) { const meta = card.querySelector('.artifact-meta span'); return meta ? meta.textContent.trim() : ''; } function getCardHerbs(card) { const tags = card.querySelectorAll('.artifact-tag'); return Array.from(tags).map(t => t.textContent.trim()).join(','); } function getCardRegion(card) { const meta = card.querySelectorAll('.artifact-meta span'); return meta.length > 1 ? meta[1].textContent.trim() : ''; } // ========== MODAL (enhanced with data) ========== function openModal(artifactId) { const artifact = getArtifactById(artifactId); currentArtifact = artifact; // Update meta grid const metaGrid = modal.querySelector('.meta-grid'); if (artifact.meta && metaGrid) { metaGrid.innerHTML = ` <div class="meta-item"><div class="meta-label">年代</div><div class="meta-value">${artifact.meta.period}</div></div> <div class="meta-item"><div class="meta-label">尺寸</div><div class="meta-value">${artifact.meta.size}</div></div> <div class="meta-item"><div class="meta-label">材质</div><div class="meta-value">${artifact.meta.material}</div></div> <div class="meta-item"><div class="meta-label">收藏编号</div><div class="meta-value">${artifact.meta.catalog}</div></div> `; const modal = document.getElementById('modal'); modal.classList.remove('show'); document.body.style.overflow = ''; } // ========== IMAGE NAVIGATION (P1) ========== function updateImageNav() { const controls = document.querySelector('.modal-img-controls'); } function navigateImage(dir) { const total = currentModalImages.length; currentModalImageIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(total - 1, currentModalImageIndex + dir)); updateImageNav(); showToast('切换至：' + currentModalImages[currentModalImageIndex], 'info'); } // ========== PAINTING ANNOTATIONS (P0 - enhanced click interaction) ========== function setupPaintingAnnotations(artifact) { const annotations = document.querySelectorAll('.painting-annotation'); const annotationPopup = document.getElementById('annotationPopup'); document.querySelector('.modal-img'); if (!imgBox) return; if (!document.fullscreenElement) { imgBox.requestFullscreen().catch(() => showToast('浏览器不支持全屏', 'warning')); } else { document.exitFullscreen(); } } // ========== 3D ROTATION (P2-13) ========== let is3DRotating = false; let rotation3DAngle = 0; let rotation3DRAF = null; function toggle3DRotation() { const imgBox = document.querySelector('.modal-img'); const svg = imgBox ? imgBox.querySelector('svg') : null; if (!imgBox || !svg) { showToast('请先打开文物详情', 'info'); return; } is3DRotating = !is3DRotating; if (is3DRotating) { imgBox.style.perspective = '800px'; svg.style.transformStyle = 'preserve-3d'; svg.style.transition = 'none'; showToast('3D旋转已开启，拖动鼠标旋转文物', 'success'); // Mouse drag rotation let isDragging = false; let startX = 0; let startY = 0; const onMouseDown = (e) => { isDragging = true; startX = e.clientX; startY = e.clientY; }; const onMouseMove = (e) => { if (!isDragging || !is3DRotating) return; const dx = e.clientX - startX; const dy = e.clientY - startY; rotation3DAngle += dx * 0.5; const tiltX = Math.max(-45, Math.min(45, dy * 0.3)); svg.style.transform = `rotateY(${rotation3DAngle}deg) rotateX(${-tiltX}deg)`; startX = e.clientX; startY = e.clientY; }; const onMouseUp = () => { isDragging = false; }; imgBox.addEventListener('mousedown', onMouseDown); imgBox.addEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove); imgBox.addEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp); imgBox.addEventListener('mouseleave', onMouseUp); // Store cleanup references imgBox._3dCleanup = () => { imgBox.removeEventListener('mousedown', onMouseDown); imgBox.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove); imgBox.removeEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp); imgBox.removeEventListener('mouseleave', onMouseUp); }; // Auto-rotate animation const autoRotate = () => { if (!is3DRotating) return; rotation3DAngle += 0.3; svg.style.transform = `rotateY(${rotation3DAngle}deg)`; rotation3DRAF = requestAnimationFrame(autoRotate); }; rotation3DRAF = requestAnimationFrame(autoRotate); } else { // Stop rotation if (rotation3DRAF) cancelAnimationFrame(rotation3DRAF); const svg = imgBox.querySelector('svg'); if (svg) { svg.style.transform = ''; svg.style.transition = 'transform 0.5s ease'; } if (imgBox._3dCleanup) imgBox._3dCleanup(); showToast('3D旋转已关闭', 'info'); } } // ========== RELATED RECOMMENDATIONS CLICK (P2-14) ========== document.querySelectorAll('.related-item').forEach(item => { a.name.includes(name)); if (artifact) { openModal(artifact.id); } else { // Try matching by herb name const herbRelated = ARTIFACT_DATA.find(a => a.relatedHerbs.includes(name.replace('图', ''))); if document.getElementById('modal').classList.contains('show')) navigateImage(1); }); // ========== INIT: Add data attributes to existing cards ========== (userAvatar) userAvatar.style.display = 'none'; } } function goToLogin() { window.parent.showPage('login'); } function goToProfile() { window.parent.showPage('profile'); } function goToSettings() { document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() { try { if (typeof AMap === 'undefined') { showErrorMessage('高德地图 API 加载失败，请检查网络连接'); return; } initMap(); setupEventListeners(); loadAncientOverlay(); } catch (e) { console.error('初始化失败:', e); showErrorMessage('地图初始化失败: ' + e.message); } }); function showErrorMessage(msg) { const loading = document.getElementById('loadingScreen'); if (loading) { loading.innerHTML = ` <div class="loading-content"> <div style="font-size:48px;margin-bottom:16px">⚠️</div> <div style="font-size:16px;color:#ff4d4f;margin-bottom:12px">${msg}</div> <div style="font-size:14px;color:var(--txt3)">请刷新页面重试</div> </div> `; } } function initMap() { try { // 主地图 - 现代底图 map = new AMap.Map('map', { zoom: 5, center: [112.0, 35.0], viewMode: '2D', mapStyle: 'amap://styles/whitesmoke', features: ['bg', 'building', 'point'] }); // 左侧地图（卷帘模式） mapLeft = new AMap.Map('mapLeft', { zoom: 5, center: [112.0, 35.0], viewMode: '2D', mapStyle: 'amap://styles/whitesmoke' }); // 同步两个地图 syncMaps(); // 加载古地图叠加层 setTimeout(() => { try { loadAncientOverlay(); const img = document.getElementById('ancientMapImg'); if (img) img.style.opacity = '0.7'; addMarkers(); addRoutes(); // 初始化底部时间轴状态 document.querySelectorAll('.timeline-period').forEach(period => { period.classList.toggle('active', period.dataset.period === 'northern'); }); } catch (e) { console.error('加载叠加层失败:', e); } }, 500); // 隐藏加载层 setTimeout(() => { const loading = document.getElementById('loadingScreen'); if (loading) loading.classList.add('hidden'); }, 1000); } catch (e) { console.error('地图初始化失败:', e); throw e; } } function syncMaps() { // 同步地图移动和缩放 if (!map || !mapLeft) { console.warn('地图未初始化，跳过同步'); return; } map.on('moveend', () => { if (compareMode === 'curtain' && mapLeft) { try { const center = map.getCenter(); const zoom = map.getZoom(); mapLeft.setZoomAndCenter(zoom, [center.lng, center.lat]); } catch (e) { console.error('同步地图移动失败:', e); } } }); map.on('zoomend', () => { if (compareMode === 'curtain' && mapLeft) { try { const center = map.getCenter(); const zoom = map.getZoom(); mapLeft.setZoomAndCenter(zoom, [center.lng, center.lat]); } catch (e) { console.error('同步地图缩放失败:', e); } } }); } // ========== ANCIENT MAP OVERLAY ========== let ancientImageOverlay = null; function loadAncientOverlay() { // 获取当前朝代对应的古地图图片 const imgUrl = currentDynasty === 'northern' ? '北宋地图.jpg' : '南宋地图.jpg'; const bounds = ANCIENT_MAP_BOUNDS[currentDynasty]; // 使用图片叠加层 - 在透明度模式下显示在地图上方 const img = document.getElementById('ancientMapImg'); img.src = imgUrl; // 创建高德地图图片图层 (ImageLayer) try { // 移除旧图层 if (ancientImageOverlay) { try { map.remove(ancientImageOverlay); } catch (e) { console.log('移除旧图层失败:', e); } ancientImageOverlay = null; } // 获取西南角和东北角的坐标 const southWest = new AMap.LngLat(bounds.southwest[0], bounds.southwest[1]); const northEast = new AMap.LngLat(bounds.northeast[0], bounds.northeast[1]); const imageBounds = new AMap.Bounds(southWest, northEast); // 创建图片图层 - 使用 options 对象形式 ancientImageOverlay = new AMap.ImageLayer({ url: imgUrl, bounds: imageBounds, opacity: 0.7, zooms: [3, 18], visible: true }); // 添加到地图 ancientImageOverlay.setMap(map); console.log('古地图叠加层已加载:', imgUrl); } catch (e) { console.error('加载高德地图图片图层失败:', e); // 失败时仅使用 DOM 图片叠加 } // 更新卷帘模式的古地图图片 document.getElementById('curtainAncientImg').src = imgUrl; } function updateAncientOverlay() { loadAncientOverlay(); } // ========== DYNASTY SWITCHING ========== function switchDynasty(dynasty) { if (currentDynasty === dynasty) return; currentDynasty = dynasty; // 更新顶部按钮状态 document.querySelectorAll('.dynasty-btn').forEach(btn => { btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.dynasty === dynasty); }); // 更新底部时间轴状态 document.querySelectorAll('.timeline-period').forEach(period => { period.classList.toggle('active', period.dataset.period === dynasty); }); try { // 更新古地图叠加层 updateAncientOverlay(); // 更新标记 clearMarkers(); addMarkers(); addRoutes(); // 调整地图视图 if (map) { if (dynasty === 'southern') { map.setCenter([115.0, 30.0]); map.setZoom(6); } else { map.setCenter([112.0, 35.0]); map.setZoom(5); } } showToast(`已切换至${dynasty === 'northern' ? '北宋' : '南宋'}地图`, 'success'); } catch (e) { console.error('切换朝代失败:', e); showToast('切换地图失败，请刷新页面重试', 'error'); } } // ========== COMPARE MODE ========== function switchCompareMode(mode) { if (compareMode === mode) return; compareMode = mode; // 更新标签状态 document.querySelectorAll('.compare-tab').forEach(tab => { tab.classList.toggle('active', tab.dataset.mode === mode); }); const mapContainer = document.getElementById('mapContainer'); const curtainContainer = document.getElementById('curtainContainer'); const opacitySliderContainer = document.getElementById('opacitySliderContainer'); if (mode === 'curtain') { // 切换到卷帘模式 mapContainer.style.display = 'none'; document.getElementById('ancientOverlay').style.display = 'none'; // 隐藏透明度模式的叠加层 curtainContainer.classList.add('active'); opacitySliderContainer.style.display = 'none'; // 同步地图位置 const center = map.getCenter(); const zoom = map.getZoom(); mapLeft.setZoomAndCenter(zoom, [center.lng, center.lat]); } else { // 切换到透明度模式 mapContainer.style.display = 'block'; document.getElementById('ancientOverlay').style.display = 'block'; // 显示叠加层 curtainContainer.classList.remove('active'); opacitySliderContainer.style.display = 'block'; } showToast(`已切换至${mode === 'curtain' ? '卷帘对比' : '透明度混合'}模式`, 'success'); } // 卷帘拖动 function setupCurtainDrag() { const divider = document.getElementById('curtainDivider'); const container = document.getElementById('curtainContainer'); const modern = document.getElementById('curtainModern'); const ancient = document.getElementById('curtainAncient'); if (!divider || !container) return; let isDragging = false; divider.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => { isDragging = true; e.preventDefault(); divider.style.cursor = 'col-resize'; }); document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => { if (!isDragging) return; const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect(); const x = e.clientX - rect.left; let percentage = (x / rect.width) * 100; // 限制范围 20% - 80% percentage = Math.max(20, Math.min(80, percentage)); curtainPosition = percentage; // 更新分割位置 modern.style.flex = `0 0 ${percentage}%`; ancient.style.flex = `0 0 ${100 - percentage}%`; divider.style.left = `${percentage}%`; }); document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => { isDragging = false; if (divider) divider.style.cursor = 'ew-resize'; }); // 触摸支持 divider.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => { isDragging = true; e.preventDefault(); }); document.addEventListener('touchmove', (e) => { if (!isDragging) return; const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect(); const touch = e.touches[0]; const x = touch.clientX - rect.left; let percentage = (x / rect.width) * 100; percentage = Math.max(20, Math.min(80, percentage)); curtainPosition = percentage; modern.style.flex = `0 0 ${percentage}%`; ancient.style.flex = `0 0 ${100 - percentage}%`; divider.style.left = `${percentage}%`; }); document.addEventListener('touchend', () => { isDragging = false; }); } // ========== MARKERS & ROUTES ========== function addMarkers() { if (!map) return; const locations = currentDynasty === 'northern' ? NORTHERN_SONG_LOCATIONS : SOUTHERN_SONG_LOCATIONS; // 古代标记（药囊图标） if (layerStates.ancient) { locations.forEach(loc => { try { const marker = new AMap.Marker({ position: loc.pos, title: loc.name, icon: new AMap.Icon({ size: new AMap.Size(32, 32), image: createAncientMarkerIcon(), imageSize: new AMap.Size(32, 32) }), offset: new AMap.Pixel(-16, -16), extData: { type: 'ancient', data: loc } }); marker.on('click', () => showLocationInfo(loc, 'ancient')); map.add(marker); markers.ancient.push(marker); } catch (e) { console.error('添加古代标记失败:', e); } }); } // 现代标记（圆点图标） if (layerStates.modern) { MODERN_LOCATIONS.forEach(loc => { try { const marker = new AMap.Marker({ position: loc.pos, title: loc.name, icon: new AMap.Icon({ size: new AMap.Size(20, 20), image: createModernMarkerIcon(), imageSize: new AMap.Size(20, 20) }), offset: new AMap.Pixel(-10, -10), extData: { type: 'modern', data: loc } }); marker.on('click', () => showLocationInfo(loc, 'modern')); map.add(marker); markers.modern.push(marker); // 同步添加到左侧地图 if (compareMode === 'curtain' && mapLeft) { const leftMarker = marker.clone(); mapLeft.add(leftMarker); } } catch (e) { console.error('添加现代标记失败:', e); } }); } } function clearMarkers() { if (!map) return; markers.ancient.forEach(m => { try { map.remove(m); } catch(e) {} }); markers.modern.forEach(m => { try { map.remove(m); } catch(e) {} }); markers.ancient = []; markers.modern = []; } function addRoutes() { if (!map || !layerStates.routes) return; const locations = currentDynasty === 'northern' ? NORTHERN_SONG_LOCATIONS : SOUTHERN_SONG_LOCATIONS; // 绘制古今产地变迁连线 locations.forEach(ancient => { try { const modern = MODERN_LOCATIONS.find(m => ancient.herbs.some(h => m.herbs.includes(h)) ); if (modern && ancient.pos && modern.pos) { const polyline = new AMap.Polyline({ path: [ancient.pos, modern.pos], strokeColor: '#00d4aa', strokeWeight: 2, strokeStyle: 'dashed', strokeDasharray: [8, 4], extData: { ancient: ancient.name, modern: modern.name } }); polyline.on('click', () => { showToast(`${ancient.name} → ${modern.name}`, 'info'); }); map.add(polyline); polylines.push(polyline); } } catch (e) { console.error('绘制连线失败:', e); } }); } function clearRoutes() { if (!map) return; polylines.forEach(p => { try { map.remove(p); } catch(e) {} }); polylines = []; } // ========== ICONS ========== function createAncientMarkerIcon() { // 创建药囊SVG图标 const svg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 32 32"> <defs> <filter id="glow" x="-50%" y="-50%" width="200%" height="200%"> <feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="2" result="coloredBlur"/> <feMerge><feMergeNode in="coloredBlur"/><feMergeNode in="SourceGraphic"/></feMerge> </filter> </defs> <circle cx="16" cy="16" r="14" fill="#c9b037" filter="url(#glow)"/> <circle cx="16" cy="16" r="10" fill="#1a1e28"/> <text x="16" y="20" text-anchor="middle" fill="#c9b037" font-size="12" font-family="Noto Serif SC">药</text> </svg>`; return 'data:image/svg+xml;base64,' + btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent(svg))); } function createModernMarkerIcon() { // 创建现代圆点SVG图标 const svg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 20 20"> <defs> <filter id="glow" x="-50%" y="-50%" width="200%" height="200%"> <feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="1.5" result="coloredBlur"/> <feMerge><feMergeNode in="coloredBlur"/><feMergeNode in="SourceGraphic"/></feMerge> </filter> </defs> <circle cx="10" cy="10" r="8" fill="#00d4aa" filter="url(#glow)"/> <circle cx="10" cy="10" r="4" fill="#fff"/> </svg>`; return 'data:image/svg+xml;base64,' + btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent(svg))); } // ========== INFO PANEL ========== function showLocationInfo(loc, type) { const panel = document.getElementById('infoPanel'); document.getElementById('infoTitle').textContent = type === 'ancient' ? loc.name : loc.name; document.getElementById('infoSubtitle').textContent = type === 'ancient' ? `今${loc.modern}` : '现代道地药材产区'; // 置信度 const confidenceEl = document.getElementById('infoConfidence'); if (type === 'ancient' && loc.confidence) { confidenceEl.style.display = 'inline-flex'; confidenceEl.className = `confidence-badge ${loc.confidence}`; confidenceEl.innerHTML = `<span>${loc.confidence === 'high' ? '✓' : loc.confidence === 'medium' ? '∼' : '?'}</span> ${loc.confidence === 'high' ? '高' : loc.confidence === 'medium' ? '中' : '低'}置信度定位`; } else { confidenceEl.style.display = 'none'; } // 坐标 document.getElementById('coordLng').textContent = loc.pos[0].toFixed(2) + '°E'; document.getElementById('coordLat').textContent = loc.pos[1].toFixed(2) + '°N'; // 时间轴 const timelineEl = document.getElementById('infoTimeline'); if (loc.timeline) { timelineEl.innerHTML = loc.timeline.map(t => ` <div class="timeline-item"> <div class="timeline-era">${t.era}</div> <div class="timeline-text">${t.text}</div> </div> `).join(''); } // 相关药材 const herbsEl = document.getElementById('infoHerbs'); if (loc.herbs && loc.herbs.length > 0) { herbsEl.innerHTML = ` <div class="herbs-title">📚 相关药物（${loc.herbs.length}）</div> <div class="herb-tags"> ${loc.herbs.map(h => `<span class="herb-tag" onclick="location.href='duizhao-detail.html?herb=${h}'">${h}</span>`).join('')} </div> `; } panel.classList.add('show'); // 飞行动画 if (map && loc.pos) { map.setZoomAndCenter(8, loc.pos, true, 1000); } } function closeInfoPanel() { document.getElementById('infoPanel').classList.remove('show'); } // ========== EVENT LISTENERS ========== function setupEventListeners() { // 透明度滑块 const opacitySlider = document.getElementById('opacitySlider'); const sliderValue = document.getElementById('sliderValue'); opacitySlider.addEventListener('input', (e) => { const value = e.target.value; const opacity = value / 100; // 控制 DOM 图片叠加层的透明度（主要方式） const img = document.getElementById('ancientMapImg'); if (img) { img.style.opacity = opacity; } // 尝试控制高德地图 ImageLayer 的透明度（如果支持） if (ancientImageOverlay) { try { if (typeof ancientImageOverlay.setOpacity === 'function') { ancientImageOverlay.setOpacity(opacity); } else if (ancientImageOverlay.setOptions) { // 通过 setOptions 更新透明度 ancientImageOverlay.setOptions({ opacity: opacity }); } } catch (e) { // 忽略错误，DOM 方式已经足够 } } document.getElementById('labelModern').classList.toggle('active', value < 30); document.getElementById('labelAncient').classList.toggle('active', value > 50); sliderValue.textContent = `古地图透明度 ${value}%`; }); // 图层切换 document.querySelectorAll('.layer-toggle').forEach(toggle => { toggle.addEventListener('click', function() { this.classList.toggle('active'); const layer = this.dataset.layer; const isActive = this.classList.contains('active'); layerStates[layer] = isActive; if (layer === 'heatmap') { toggleHeatmap(isActive); } else { clearMarkers(); clearRoutes(); addMarkers(); addRoutes(); } }); }); // 卷帘拖动 setupCurtainDrag(); // 缩放控制 document.getElementById('zoomIn').addEventListener('click', () => { map.zoomIn(); }); document.getElementById('zoomOut').addEventListener('click', () => { map.zoomOut(); }); // 搜索 setupSearch(); } // ========== SEARCH ========== function setupSearch() { const searchInput = document.getElementById('searchInput'); const suggestions = document.getElementById('searchSuggestions'); searchInput.addEventListener('input', (e) => { const query = e.target.value.trim().toLowerCase(); if (query.length === 0) { suggestions.classList.remove('show'); return; } const locations = currentDynasty === 'northern' ? NORTHERN_SONG_LOCATIONS : SOUTHERN_SONG_LOCATIONS; const results = locations.filter(loc => loc.name.includes(query) || loc.modern.includes(query) || loc.herbs.some(h => h.includes(query)) ); if (results.length > 0) { suggestions.innerHTML = results.map(loc => ` <div class="suggestion-item" onclick="selectLocation('${loc.id}')"> <div class="suggestion-icon">📍</div> <div class="suggestion-info"> <div class="suggestion-name">${loc.name}</div> <div class="suggestion-desc">${loc.modern} · ${loc.herbs.join('、')}</div> </div> </div> `).join(''); suggestions.classList.add('show'); } else { suggestions.innerHTML = '<div style="padding:12px;color:var(--txt3);text-align:center">未找到相关结果</div>'; suggestions.classList.add('show'); } }); document.addEventListener('click', (e) => { if (!e.target.closest('.search-panel')) { suggestions.classList.remove('show'); } }); } function selectLocation(id) { const locations = currentDynasty === 'northern' ? NORTHERN_SONG_LOCATIONS : SOUTHERN_SONG_LOCATIONS; const loc = locations.find(l => l.id === id); if (loc) { document.getElementById('searchInput').value = loc.name; document.getElementById('searchSuggestions').classList.remove('show'); showLocationInfo(loc, 'ancient'); } } // ========== UTILITIES ========== // ========== BASEMAP SWITCHING ========== let currentBasemap = 'whitesmoke'; function switchBasemap(basemap) { currentBasemap = basemap; // 更新按钮样式 document.querySelectorAll('.basemap-btn').forEach(btn => { const isActive = btn.dataset.basemap === basemap; btn.classList.toggle('active', isActive); btn.style.background = isActive ? 'rgba(0,212,170,0.1)' : 'transparent'; btn.style.borderColor = isActive ? 'var(--cyan)' : 'transparent'; btn.style.color = isActive ? 'var(--cyan)' : 'var(--txt2)'; }); // 切换地图样式 if (map) { switch(basemap) { case 'satellite': map.setMapStyle('amap://styles/satellite'); if (mapLeft) mapLeft.setMapStyle('amap://styles/satellite'); showToast('已切换至卫星图', 'success'); break; case 'normal': map.setMapStyle('amap://styles/normal'); if (mapLeft) mapLeft.setMapStyle('amap://styles/normal'); showToast('已切换至道路图', 'success'); break; case 'whitesmoke': default: map.setMapStyle('amap://styles/whitesmoke'); if (mapLeft) mapLeft.setMapStyle('amap://styles/whitesmoke'); showToast('已切换至水墨风格', 'success'); break; } } } // ========== FULLSCREEN MODE ========== let isFullscreen = false; function toggleFullscreen() { if (!document.fullscreenElement) { document.documentElement.requestFullscreen().then(() => { isFullscreen = true; document.body.classList.add('fullscreen'); showToast('已进入全屏模式，按ESC退出', 'success'); }).catch(err => { console.error('全屏模式失败:', err); }); } else { document.exitFullscreen().then(() => { isFullscreen = false; document.body.classList.remove('fullscreen'); }); } } // 监听全屏变化 document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', () => { if (!document.fullscreenElement) { isFullscreen = false; document.body.classList.remove('fullscreen'); } }); // ========== HEATMAP ========== function toggleHeatmap(show) { if (!map) return; if (show) { // 加载热力图数据 const locations = currentDynasty === 'northern' ? NORTHERN_SONG_LOCATIONS : SOUTHERN_SONG_LOCATIONS; // 构建热力图数据点 const heatmapData = locations.map(loc => ({ lng: loc.pos[0], lat: loc.pos[1], count: loc.herbs.length * 10 // 根据药材数量加权 })); // 使用高德热力图插件 if (typeof AMap.HeatMap !== 'undefined') { if (!heatmapLayer) { heatmapLayer = new AMap.HeatMap(map, { radius: 25, opacity: [0, 0.8], gradient: { 0.5: 'blue', 0.65: 'rgb(117,211,248)', 0.7: 'rgb(0, 255, 0)', 0.9: '#ffea00', 1.0: 'red' } }); } heatmapLayer.setDataSet({ data: heatmapData, max: 30 }); heatmapLayer.show(); showToast('热力图已开启', 'success'); } else { // 如果没有热力图插件，使用圆形覆盖物模拟 showSimpleHeatmap(heatmapData); } } else { if (heatmapLayer) { heatmapLayer.hide(); } hideSimpleHeatmap(); } } // 简化版热力图（使用圆形覆盖物） let simpleHeatmapCircles = []; function showSimpleHeatmap(data) { hideSimpleHeatmap(); data.forEach(point => { const circle = new AMap.Circle({ center: [point.lng, point.lat], radius: 50000 + point.count * 5000, // 半径根据权重调整 fillColor: '#ff4d4f', fillOpacity: 0.3, strokeColor: '#ff7875', strokeWeight: 1, strokeOpacity: 0.5 }); map.add(circle); simpleHeatmapCircles.push(circle); }); } function hideSimpleHeatmap() { simpleHeatmapCircles.forEach(circle => map.remove(circle)); simpleHeatmapCircles = []; } // ========== DRUG-SPECIFIC HEATMAP ========== function showDrugHeatmap(drugName) { if (!map) return; // 查找该药物的所有产地 const locations = currentDynasty === 'northern' ? NORTHERN_SONG_LOCATIONS : SOUTHERN_SONG_LOCATIONS; const drugLocations = locations.filter(loc => loc.herbs.some(h => h.includes(drugName)) ); if (drugLocations.length === 0) { showToast(`未找到${drugName}的产地数据`, 'error'); return; } // 构建热力图数据 const heatmapData = drugLocations.map(loc => ({ lng: loc.pos[0], lat: loc.pos[1], count: 20 })); // 高亮显示这些产地 drugLocations.forEach(loc => { const marker = new AMap.CircleMarker({ center: loc.pos, radius: 15, fillColor: '#ff4d4f', fillOpacity: 0.8, strokeColor: '#fff', strokeWeight: 2 }); map.add(marker); // 3秒后移除高亮 setTimeout(() => map.remove(marker), 3000); }); // 自适应视野 map.setFitView(); showToast(`${drugName}产地分布已高亮`, 'success'); } function resetView() { if (!map) { showToast('地图尚未加载完成', 'error'); return; } map.setZoomAndCenter(5, [112.0, 35.0]); closeInfoPanel(); showToast('视图已重置', 'success'); } function showToast(message, type = 'info') { const container = document.getElementById('toastContainer'); const toast = document.createElement('div'); toast.className = `toast ${type}`; toast.textContent = message; container.appendChild(toast); setTimeout(() => { toast.remove(); }, 3000); } // ========== User Menu ========== function initUserMenu() { const isLoggedIn = localStorage.getItem('isLoggedIn'); const loginBtn = document.getElementById('loginBtn'); const userAvatar = document.getElementById('userAvatar'); const navUserAvatar = document.getElementById('navUserAvatar'); if (isLoggedIn === 'true') { const user = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('benCaoUser') || '{}'); if (loginBtn) loginBtn.style.display = 'none'; if (userAvatar) { userAvatar.style.display = 'flex'; if (navUserAvatar) navUserAvatar.src = user.avatar || ''; } } else { if (loginBtn) loginBtn.style.display = 'block'; if (userAvatar) userAvatar.style.display = 'none'; } } function goToProfile() { window.parent.showPage('profile'); } function goToLogin() { window.parent.showPage('login'); } function toggleUserDropdown() { const dd = document.getElementById('userDropdown'); if (dd) dd.classList.toggle('show'); } function logout() { localStorage.removeItem('isLoggedIn'); localStorage.removeItem('benCaoUser'); window.location.reload(); } // 点击外部关闭下拉 window.addEventListener('click', function(e) { const avatar = document.getElementById('userAvatar'); const dd = document.getElementById('userDropdown'); if (dd && avatar && !avatar.contains(e.target) && !dd.contains(e.target)) { dd.classList.remove('show'); } }); // 页面加载时初始化用户菜单 initUserMenu(); </script> </body> </html> ﻿<!DOCTYPE html> <html lang="zh-CN"> <head> <meta charset="UTF-8"> <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0"> mouseY = 0; let glowX = 0, glowY = 0; const hasFinePointer = window.matchMedia('(pointer: fine)').matches; if (hasFinePointer) { document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => { mouseX = e.clientX; mouseY = e.clientY; mouseGlow.style.opacity = '1'; }); document.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => { mouseGlow.style.opacity = '0'; }); function animateGlow() { glowX += (mouseX - glowX) * 0.1; inner.className = 'annot-spot-inner'; spot.appendChild(inner); const tooltip = document.createElement('div'); tooltip.className = 'annot-tooltip'; tooltip.innerHTML = `<b>${partEmojis[key] || '🌿'} ${part.name}</b><br/><span style="color:var(--cyan);font-size:11px">古：${part.ancient.slice(0,10)}… · 相似度 ${part.similarity}%</span>`; spot.appendChild(tooltip); // 点击圈选 → 同步高亮右栏 + 滚动到对应部位 spot.addEventListener('click', (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); highlightPart(key); // 滚动右栏对应部位到可视区 const rightItem = document.querySelector(`.detail-part-item[data-part="${key}"]`); if (rightItem) rightItem.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' }); }); // 鼠标悬浮 → 高亮 spot.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => { highlightPart(key); }); layer.appendChild(spot); }); wrap.appendChild(layer); } function showViewerImage(canvasId, imgList, state, captionIds) { if (!imgList.length) return; state.idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(state.idx, imgList.length - 1)); const img = imgList[state.idx]; state.zoom = 1; state.panX = 0; state.panY = 0; updateViewerTransform(canvasId, state); const canvas = document.getElementById(canvasId); const existing = canvas.querySelector('.placeholder-svg'); if (existing) existing.remove(); const wrap = document.createElement('div'); wrap.className = 'placeholder-svg'; // 如果有真实图片URL，优先显示图片；否则用SVG占位 if (img.url) { const imgEl = document.createElement('img'); imgEl.src = img.url; imgEl.alt = img.title || '药材图'; imgEl.style.cssText = 'width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:contain;display:block;'; imgEl.onerror = function() { // 图片加载失败时回退到SVG wrap.innerHTML = generateSVG(img, false); }; wrap.appendChild(imgEl); } else { wrap.innerHTML = generateSVG(img, false); } canvas.insertBefore(wrap, canvas.firstChild); // Update caption if (captionIds.title) document.getElementById(captionIds.title).textContent = img.title; if (captionIds.source) document.getElementById(captionIds.source).textContent = img.source; if (captionIds.note) document.getElementById(captionIds.note).textContent = img.caption; if (captionIds.version) document.getElementById(captionIds.version).textContent = img.version; // Rebuild annotations buildAnnotations(canvasId, state); } // Transform helper function updateViewerTransform(canvasId, state) { const wrap = document.querySelector(`#${canvasId} .placeholder-svg`); if (!wrap) return; wrap.style.transform = `translate(${state.panX}px, ${state.panY}px) scale(${state.zoom})`; const zoomIndId = canvasId === 'ancCanvas' ? 'ancZoomInd' : 'modZoomInd'; const ind = document.getElementById(zoomIndId); if (ind) ind.textContent = Math.round(state.zoom * 100) + '%'; } // Setup canvas interactions (wheel zoom + drag + touch) function setupCanvasInteractions(canvasId, state, imgList, captionIds) { const canvas = document.getElementById(canvasId); canvas.addEventListener('wheel', (e) => { e.preventDefault(); const delta = e.deltaY > 0 ? -0.12 : 0.12; state.zoom = Math.max(0.3, Math.min(5, state.zoom + delta)); updateViewerTransform(canvasId, state); }, { passive: false }); canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => { if (e.target.closest('.annotation-marker')) return; state.dragging = true; state.dragStartX = e.clientX - state.panX; state.dragStartY = e.clientY - state.panY; canvas.classList.add('dragging'); }); document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => { if (!state.dragging) return; state.panX = e.clientX - state.dragStartX; state.panY = e.clientY - state.dragStartY; updateViewerTransform(canvasId, state); }); document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => { state.dragging = false; canvas.classList.remove('dragging'); }); // Touch let lastDist = 0; canvas.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => { if (e.touches.length === 1) { state.dragging = true; state.dragStartX = e.touches[0].clientX - state.panX; state.dragStartY = e.touches[0].clientY - state.panY; } else if (e.touches.length === 2) { state.dragging = false; lastDist = Math.hypot(e.touches[0].clientX - e.touches[1].clientX, e.touches[0].clientY - e.touches[1].clientY); } }, { passive: true }); canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', (e) => { e.preventDefault(); if (e.touches.length === 1 && state.dragging) { state.panX = e.touches[0].clientX - state.dragStartX; state.panY = e.touches[0].clientY - state.dragStartY; updateViewerTransform(canvasId, state); } else if (e.touches.length === 2) { const dist = Math.hypot(e.touches[0].clientX - e.touches[1].clientX, e.touches[0].clientY - e.touches[1].clientY); state.zoom = Math.max(0.3, Math.min(5, state.zoom * (dist / lastDist))); lastDist = dist; updateViewerTransform(canvasId, state); } }, { passive: false }); canvas.addEventListener('touchend', () => { state.dragging = false; }); } // Render thumbnails for a viewer function renderThumbnails(stripId, imgList, state, canvasId, captionIds) { const strip = document.getElementById(stripId); strip.innerHTML = ''; imgList.forEach((img, idx) => { const thumb = document.createElement('div'); thumb.className = 'thumb-item' + (idx === 0 ? ' active' : ''); // 缩略图：有真实图片用img，否则用SVG if (img.url) { const imgEl = document.createElement('img'); imgEl.src = img.url; imgEl.alt = img.title; imgEl.style.cssText = 'width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;border-radius:4px;'; imgEl.onerror = function() { this.outerHTML = generateSVG(img, true); }; thumb.appendChild(imgEl); } else { thumb.innerHTML = generateSVG(img, true); } const label = document.createElement('span'); label.className = 'thumb-label'; label.textContent = img.title.slice(-4); thumb.appendChild(label); thumb.addEventListener('click', () => { state.idx = idx; showViewerImage(canvasId, imgList, state, captionIds); strip.querySelectorAll('.thumb-item').forEach((t, i) => t.classList.toggle('active', i === idx)); }); strip.appendChild(thumb); }); } // Caption ID maps const ancCaptionIds = { title: 'ancCapTitle', source: 'ancCapSource', note: 'ancCapNote', version: 'ancCapVersion' }; const modCaptionIds = { title: 'modCapTitle', source: 'modCapSource', note: 'modCapNote', version: 'modCapVersion' }; // Initialize both viewers setupCanvasInteractions('ancCanvas', ancState, ancientImages, ancCaptionIds); setupCanvasInteractions('modCanvas', modState, modernImages, modCaptionIds); if (ancientImages.length > 0) { renderThumbnails('ancThumbs', ancientImages, ancState, 'ancCanvas', ancCaptionIds); showViewerImage('ancCanvas', ancientImages, ancState, ancCaptionIds); } else { document.getElementById('ancCapTitle').textContent = '暂无古图'; } if (modernImages.length > 0) { renderThumbnails('modThumbs', modernImages, modState, 'modCanvas', modCaptionIds); showViewerImage('modCanvas', modernImages, modState, modCaptionIds); } else { document.getElementById('modCapTitle').textContent = '暂无今照'; } // Toolbar buttons — Ancient document.getElementById('ancZoomIn').addEventListener('click', () => { ancState.zoom = Math.min(5, ancState.zoom + 0.3); updateViewerTransform('ancCanvas', ancState); }); document.getElementById('ancZoomOut').addEventListener('click', () => { ancState.zoom = Math.max(0.3, ancState.zoom - 0.3); updateViewerTransform('ancCanvas', ancState); }); document.getElementById('ancReset').addEventListener('click', () => { ancState.zoom = 1; ancState.panX = 0; ancState.panY = 0; updateViewerTransform('ancCanvas', ancState); }); document.getElementById('ancAnnot').addEventListener('click', function() { ancState.annotVisible = !ancState.annotVisible; this.classList.toggle('active', ancState.annotVisible); buildAnnotations('ancCanvas', ancState); }); // Toolbar buttons — Modern document.getElementById('modZoomIn').addEventListener('click', () => { modState.zoom = Math.min(5, modState.zoom + 0.3); updateViewerTransform('modCanvas', modState); }); document.getElementById('modZoomOut').addEventListener('click', () => { modState.zoom = Math.max(0.3, modState.zoom - 0.3); updateViewerTransform('modCanvas', modState); }); document.getElementById('modReset').addEventListener('click', () => { modState.zoom = 1; modState.panX = 0; modState.panY = 0; updateViewerTransform('modCanvas', modState); }); document.getElementById('modAnnot').addEventListener('click', function() { modState.annotVisible = !modState.annotVisible; this.classList.toggle('active', modState.annotVisible); buildAnnotations('modCanvas', modState); }); // ========== 细节对照区（右栏） ========== const detailParts = document.getElementById('detailParts'); const partEmojis = { root: '🥔', leaf: '🌿', flower: '🌸', stem: '🌱' }; const partAncientDescs = { root: '《图经》云：「根如人形者神」，以根之形态为鉴别要诀', leaf: '《图经》绘叶为掌状复叶，古籍多云「叶五出」', flower: '《图经》绘花顶生伞形，古注「花细小，黄绿色」', stem: '《图经》绘茎直立，古注「茎有细纵棱」' }; const partModernDescs = { root: '现代观察：主根肥大，肉质，圆柱形或纺锤形，下部有分枝', leaf: '现代观察：掌状复叶，小叶3-5，边缘有细锯齿', flower: '现代观察：伞形花序顶生，花小，黄绿色，花瓣5', stem: '现代观察：茎直立，圆柱形，光滑无毛' }; const partDiffs = { root: null, leaf: '古图绘叶对生而现代观察为掌状复叶互生，存在差异', flower: null, stem: null }; Object.entries(herb.parts).forEach(([key, part], index) => { const sim = part.similarity; const simClass = sim >= 85 ? 'high' : sim >= 60 ? 'mid' : 'low'; const diff = partDiffs[key]; const item = document.createElement('div'); item.className = 'detail-part-item' + (index === 0 ? '同步高亮：开' : '同步高亮：关'; this.style.color = syncHighlight ? 'var(--cyan)' : 'var(--txt3)'; this.style.borderColor = syncHighlight ? 'var(--cyan-glow)' : 'var(--glass-border)'; }); // Diff alert expand document.querySelectorAll('.diff-alert').forEach(alert => { alert.addEventListener('click', function(e) { e.stopPropagation(); this.classList.toggle('expanded'); const expand = this.querySelector('.diff-expand'); if (this.classList.contains('expanded')) { expand.textContent = '收起 ▲'; this.querySelector('.diff-text').textContent = '存疑：' + (partDiffs[this.dataset.part] || '').slice(0, 40) + '...'; } else { expand.textContent = '展开 ▼'; } }); }); // Auto-select first part if (Object.keys(herb.parts).length > 0) { activePartKey = Object.keys(herb.parts)[0]; highlightPart(activePartKey); } // ========== Render Evidence List — 默认显示前3条 ========== const evidenceList = document.getElementById('evidenceList'); const MAX_VISIBLE_EVIDENCE = 3; herb.evidence.forEach((ev, idx) => { const card = document.createElement('div'); card.className = `evidence-card ${ev.type}` + (idx >= MAX_VISIBLE_EVIDENCE ? } // ========== Tab Switching ========== const detailTabs = document.querySelectorAll('.detail-tab'); const tabContents = document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content'); detailTabs.forEach(tab => { tab.addEventListener('click', function() { const target = this.dataset.tab; }); }); // ========== Render 考证原文 Tab ========== function renderTextEvidence() { const container = document.getElementById('textEvidenceContent'); const refList = document.getElementById('refList'); const versionList = document.getElementById('versionList'); ['川芎', '川贝母', '附子', '麦冬'] }, { id: 'zhejiang', name: '浙江磐安', pos: [120.5, 29.1], herbs: ['浙贝母', '白术', '元胡'] }, { id: 'guangdong', name: '广东新会', pos: [113.0, 22.5], herbs: ['陈皮', '砂仁', '肉桂'] }, { id: 'yunnan', name: '云南文山', pos: [104.2, 23.4], herbs: ['三七', '天麻', '茯苓'] }, { id: 'gansu', name: '甘肃岷县', pos: [104.0, 34.4], herbs: ['当归', '黄芪', '党参'] } ]; // ========== INITIALIZATION ========== document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() { try { if (typeof AMap === 'undefined') { showErrorMessage('高德地图 API 加载失败，请检查网络连接'); return; } initMap(); setupEventListeners(); loadAncientOverlay(); } catch (e) { console.error('初始化失败:', e); showErrorMessage('地图初始化失败: ' + e.message); } }); function showErrorMessage(msg) { const loading = document.getElementById('loadingScreen'); if (loading) { loading.innerHTML = ` <div class="loading-content"> <div style="font-size:48px;margin-bottom:16px">\u26a0\ufe0f</div> <div style="font-size:16px;color:#ff4d4f;margin-bottom:12px">${msg}</div> <div style="font-size:14px;color:var(--txt3)">请刷新页面重试</div> </div> `; } } function initMap() { try { // 主地图 - 现代底图 map = new AMap.Map('map', { zoom: 5, center: [112.0, 35.0], viewMode: '2D', mapStyle: 'amap://styles/whitesmoke', features: ['bg', 'building', 'point'] }); // 左侧地图（卷帘模式） mapLeft = new AMap.Map('mapLeft', { zoom: 5, center: [112.0, 35.0], viewMode: '2D', mapStyle: 'amap://styles/whitesmoke' }); // 同步两个地图 syncMaps(); // 加载古地图叠加层 setTimeout(() => { try { loadAncientOverlay(); const img = document.getElementById('ancientMapImg'); if (img) img.style.opacity = '0.7'; addMarkers(); addRoutes(); // 初始化底部时间轴状态 document.querySelectorAll('.timeline-period').forEach(period => { period.classList.toggle('active', period.dataset.period === 'northern'); }); } catch (e) { console.error('加载叠加层失败:', e); } }, 500); // 隐藏加载层 setTimeout(() => { const loading = document.getElementById('loadingScreen'); if (loading) loading.classList.add('hidden'); }, 1000); } catch (e) { console.error('地图初始化失败:', e); throw e; } } function syncMaps() { // 同步地图移动和缩放 if (!map || !mapLeft) { console.warn('地图未初始化，跳过同步'); return; } map.on('moveend', () => { if (compareMode === 'curtain' && mapLeft) { try { const center = map.getCenter(); const zoom = map.getZoom(); mapLeft.setZoomAndCenter(zoom, [center.lng, center.lat]); } catch (e) { console.error('同步地图移动失败:', e); } } }); map.on('zoomend', () => { if (compareMode === 'curtain' && mapLeft) { try { const center = map.getCenter(); const zoom = map.getZoom(); mapLeft.setZoomAndCenter(zoom, [center.lng, center.lat]); } catch (e) { console.error('同步地图缩放失败:', e); } } }); } // ========== ANCIENT MAP OVERLAY ========== let ancientImageOverlay = null; function loadAncientOverlay() { // 获取当前朝代对应的古地图图片 const imgUrl = currentDynasty === 'northern' ? '北宋地图.jpg' : '南宋地图.jpg'; const bounds = ANCIENT_MAP_BOUNDS[currentDynasty]; // 使用图片叠加层 - 在透明度模式下显示在地图上方 const img = document.getElementById('ancientMapImg'); img.src = imgUrl; // 创建高德地图图片图层 (ImageLayer) try { // 移除旧图层 if (ancientImageOverlay) { try { map.remove(ancientImageOverlay); } catch (e) { console.log('移除旧图层失败:', e); } ancientImageOverlay = null; } // 获取西南角和东北角的坐标 const southWest = new AMap.LngLat(bounds.southwest[0], bounds.southwest[1]); const northEast = new AMap.LngLat(bounds.northeast[0], bounds.northeast[1]); const imageBounds = new AMap.Bounds(southWest, northEast); // 创建图片图层 - 使用 options 对象形式 ancientImageOverlay = new AMap.ImageLayer({ url: imgUrl, bounds: imageBounds, opacity: 0.7, zooms: [3, 18], visible: true }); // 添加到地图 ancientImageOverlay.setMap(map); console.log('古地图叠加层已加载:', imgUrl); } catch (e) { console.error('加载高德地图图片图层失败:', e); // 失败时仅使用 DOM 图片叠加 } // 更新卷帘模式的古地图图片 document.getElementById('curtainAncientImg').src = imgUrl; } function updateAncientOverlay() { loadAncientOverlay(); } // ========== DYNASTY SWITCHING ========== function switchDynasty(dynasty) { if (currentDynasty === dynasty) return; currentDynasty = dynasty; // 更新顶部按钮状态 document.querySelectorAll('.dynasty-btn').forEach(btn => { btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.dynasty === dynasty); }); // 更新底部时间轴状态 document.querySelectorAll('.timeline-period').forEach(period => { period.classList.toggle('active', period.dataset.period === dynasty); }); try { // 更新古地图叠加层 updateAncientOverlay(); // 更新标记 clearMarkers(); addMarkers(); addRoutes(); // 调整地图视图 if (map) { if (dynasty === 'southern') { map.setCenter([115.0, 30.0]); map.setZoom(6); } else { map.setCenter([112.0, 35.0]); map.setZoom(5); } } showToast(`已切换至${dynasty === 'northern' ? '北宋' : '南宋'}地图`, 'success'); } catch (e) { console.error('切换朝代失败:', e); showToast('切换地图失败，请刷新页面重试', 'error'); } } // ========== COMPARE MODE ========== function switchCompareMode(mode) { if (compareMode === mode) return; compareMode = mode; // 更新标签状态 document.querySelectorAll('.compare-tab').forEach(tab => { tab.classList.toggle('active', tab.dataset.mode === mode); }); const mapContainer = document.getElementById('mapContainer'); const curtainContainer = document.getElementById('curtainContainer'); const opacitySliderContainer = document.getElementById('opacitySliderContainer'); if (mode === 'curtain') { // 切换到卷帘模式 mapContainer.style.display = 'none'; document.getElementById('ancientOverlay').style.display = 'none'; // 隐藏透明度模式的叠加层 curtainContainer.classList.add('active'); opacitySliderContainer.style.display = 'none'; // 同步地图位置 const center = map.getCenter(); const zoom = map.getZoom(); mapLeft.setZoomAndCenter(zoom, [center.lng, center.lat]); } else { // 切换到透明度模式 mapContainer.style.display = 'block'; document.getElementById('ancientOverlay').style.display = 'block'; // 显示叠加层 curtainContainer.classList.remove('active'); opacitySliderContainer.style.display = 'block'; } showToast(`已切换至${mode === 'curtain' ? '卷帘对比' : '透明度混合'}模式`, 'success'); } // 卷帘拖动 function setupCurtainDrag() { const divider = document.getElementById('curtainDivider'); const container = document.getElementById('curtainContainer'); const modern = document.getElementById('curtainModern'); const ancient = document.getElementById('curtainAncient'); if (!divider || !container) return; let isDragging = false; divider.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => { isDragging = true; e.preventDefault(); divider.style.cursor = 'col-resize'; }); document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => { if (!isDragging) return; const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect(); const x = e.clientX - rect.left; let percentage = (x / rect.width) * 100; // 限制范围 20% - 80% percentage = Math.max(20, Math.min(80, percentage)); curtainPosition = percentage; // 更新分割位置 modern.style.flex = `0 0 ${percentage}%`; ancient.style.flex = `0 0 ${100 - percentage}%`; divider.style.left = `${percentage}%`; }); document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => { isDragging = false; if (divider) divider.style.cursor = 'ew-resize'; }); // 触摸支持 divider.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => { isDragging = true; e.preventDefault(); }); document.addEventListener('touchmove', (e) => { if (!isDragging) return; const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect(); const touch = e.touches[0]; const x = touch.clientX - rect.left; let percentage = (x / rect.width) * 100; percentage = Math.max(20, Math.min(80, percentage)); curtainPosition = percentage; modern.style.flex = `0 0 ${percentage}%`; ancient.style.flex = `0 0 ${100 - percentage}%`; divider.style.left = `${percentage}%`; }); document.addEventListener('touchend', () => { isDragging = false; }); } // ========== MARKERS & ROUTES ========== function addMarkers() { if (!map) return; const locations = currentDynasty === 'northern' ? NORTHERN_SONG_LOCATIONS : SOUTHERN_SONG_LOCATIONS; // 古代标记（药囊图标） if (layerStates.ancient) { locations.forEach(loc => { try { const marker = new AMap.Marker({ position: loc.pos, title: loc.name, icon: new AMap.Icon({ size: new AMap.Size(32, 32), image: createAncientMarkerIcon(), imageSize: new AMap.Size(32, 32) }), offset: new AMap.Pixel(-16, -16), extData: { type: 'ancient', data: loc } }); marker.on('click', () => showLocationInfo(loc, 'ancient')); map.add(marker); markers.ancient.push(marker); } catch (e) { console.error('添加古代标记失败:', e); } }); } // 现代标记（圆点图标） if (layerStates.modern) { MODERN_LOCATIONS.forEach(loc => { try { const marker = new AMap.Marker({ position: loc.pos, title: loc.name, icon: new AMap.Icon({ size: new AMap.Size(20, 20), image: createModernMarkerIcon(), imageSize: new AMap.Size(20, 20) }), offset: new AMap.Pixel(-10, -10), extData: { type: 'modern', data: loc } }); marker.on('click', () => showLocationInfo(loc, 'modern')); map.add(marker); markers.modern.push(marker); // 同步添加到左侧地图 if (compareMode === 'curtain' && mapLeft) { const leftMarker = marker.clone(); mapLeft.add(leftMarker); } } catch (e) { console.error('添加现代标记失败:', e); } }); } } function clearMarkers() { if (!map) return; markers.ancient.forEach(m => { try { map.remove(m); } catch(e) {} }); markers.modern.forEach(m => { try { map.remove(m); } catch(e) {} }); markers.ancient = []; markers.modern = []; } function addRoutes() { if (!map || !layerStates.routes) return; const locations = currentDynasty === 'northern' ? NORTHERN_SONG_LOCATIONS : SOUTHERN_SONG_LOCATIONS; // 绘制古今产地变迁连线 locations.forEach(ancient => { try { const modern = MODERN_LOCATIONS.find(m => ancient.herbs.some(h => m.herbs.includes(h)) ); if (modern && ancient.pos && modern.pos) { const polyline = new AMap.Polyline({ path: [ancient.pos, modern.pos], strokeColor: '#00d4aa', strokeWeight: 2, strokeStyle: 'dashed', strokeDasharray: [8, 4], extData: { ancient: ancient.name, modern: modern.name } }); polyline.on('click', () => { showToast(`${ancient.name} \u2192 ${modern.name}`, 'info'); }); map.add(polyline); polylines.push(polyline); } } catch (e) { console.error('绘制连线失败:', e); } }); } function clearRoutes() { if (!map) return; polylines.forEach(p => { try { map.remove(p); } catch(e) {} }); polylines = []; } // ========== ICONS ========== function createAncientMarkerIcon() { // 创建药囊SVG图标 const svg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 32 32"> <defs> <filter id="glow" x="-50%" y="-50%" width="200%" height="200%"> <feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="2" result="coloredBlur"/> <feMerge><feMergeNode in="coloredBlur"/><feMergeNode in="SourceGraphic"/></feMerge> </filter> </defs> <circle cx="16" cy="16" r="14" fill="#c9b037" filter="url(#glow)"/> <circle cx="16" cy="16" r="10" fill="#1a1e28"/> <text x="16" y="20" text-anchor="middle" fill="#c9b037" font-size="12" font-family="Noto Serif SC">药</text> </svg>`; return 'data:image/svg+xml;base64,' + btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent(svg))); } function createModernMarkerIcon() { // 创建现代圆点SVG图标 const svg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 20 20"> <defs> <filter id="glow" x="-50%" y="-50%" width="200%" height="200%"> <feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="1.5" result="coloredBlur"/> <feMerge><feMergeNode in="coloredBlur"/><feMergeNode in="SourceGraphic"/></feMerge> </filter> </defs> <circle cx="10" cy="10" r="8" fill="#00d4aa" filter="url(#glow)"/> <circle cx="10" cy="10" r="4" fill="#fff"/> </svg>`; return 'data:image/svg+xml;base64,' + btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent(svg))); } // ========== INFO PANEL ========== function showLocationInfo(loc, type) { const panel = document.getElementById('infoPanel'); document.getElementById('infoTitle').textContent = type === 'ancient' ? loc.name : loc.name; document.getElementById('infoSubtitle').textContent = type === 'ancient' ? `今${loc.modern}` : '现代道地药材产区'; // 置信度 const confidenceEl = document.getElementById('infoConfidence'); if (type === 'ancient' && loc.confidence) { confidenceEl.style.display = 'inline-flex'; confidenceEl.className = `confidence-badge ${loc.confidence}`; confidenceEl.innerHTML = `<span>${loc.confidence === 'high' ? '\u2713' : loc.confidence === 'medium' ? '\u223c' : '?'}</span> ${loc.confidence === 'high' ? '高' : loc.confidence === 'medium' ? '中' : '低'}置信度定位`; } else { confidenceEl.style.display = 'none'; } // 坐标 document.getElementById('coordLng').textContent = loc.pos[0].toFixed(2) + '\u00b0E'; document.getElementById('coordLat').textContent = loc.pos[1].toFixed(2) + '\u00b0N'; // 时间轴 const timelineEl = document.getElementById('infoTimeline'); if (loc.timeline) { timelineEl.innerHTML = loc.timeline.map(t => ` <div class="timeline-item"> <div class="timeline-era">${t.era}</div> <div class="timeline-text">${t.text}</div> </div> `).join(''); } // 相关药材 const herbsEl = document.getElementById('infoHerbs'); if (loc.herbs && loc.herbs.length > 0) { herbsEl.innerHTML = ` <div class="herbs-title">\U0001f4da 相关药物（${loc.herbs.length}）</div> <div class="herb-tags"> ${loc.herbs.map(h => `<span class="herb-tag" onclick="location.href='duizhao-detail.html?herb=${h}'">${h}</span>`).join('')} </div> `; } panel.classList.add('show'); // 飞行动画 if (map && loc.pos) { map.setZoomAndCenter(8, loc.pos, true, 1000); } } function closeInfoPanel() { document.getElementById('infoPanel').classList.remove('show'); } // ========== EVENT LISTENERS ========== function setupEventListeners() { // 透明度滑块 const opacitySlider = document.getElementById('opacitySlider'); const sliderValue = document.getElementById('sliderValue'); opacitySlider.addEventListener('input', (e) => { const value = e.target.value; const opacity = value / 100; // 控制 DOM 图片叠加层的透明度（主要方式） const img = document.getElementById('ancientMapImg'); if (img) { img.style.opacity = opacity; } // 尝试控制高德地图 ImageLayer 的透明度（如果支持） if (ancientImageOverlay) { try { if (typeof ancientImageOverlay.setOpacity === 'function') { ancientImageOverlay.setOpacity(opacity); } else if (ancientImageOverlay.setOptions) { // 通过 setOptions 更新透明度 ancientImageOverlay.setOptions({ opacity: opacity }); } } catch (e) { // 忽略错误，DOM 方式已经足够 } } document.getElementById('labelModern').classList.toggle('active', value < 30); document.getElementById('labelAncient').classList.toggle('active', value > 50); sliderValue.textContent = `古地图透明度 ${value}%`; }); // 图层切换 document.querySelectorAll('.layer-toggle').forEach(toggle => { toggle.addEventListener('click', function() { this.classList.toggle('active'); const layer = this.dataset.layer; const isActive = this.classList.contains('active'); layerStates[layer] = isActive; if (layer === 'heatmap') { toggleHeatmap(isActive); } else { clearMarkers(); clearRoutes(); addMarkers(); addRoutes(); } }); }); // 卷帘拖动 setupCurtainDrag(); // 缩放控制 document.getElementById('zoomIn').addEventListener('click', () => { map.zoomIn(); }); document.getElementById('zoomOut').addEventListener('click', () => { map.zoomOut(); }); // 搜索 setupSearch(); } // ========== SEARCH ========== function setupSearch() { const searchInput = document.getElementById('searchInput'); const suggestions = document.getElementById('searchSuggestions'); searchInput.addEventListener('input', (e) => { const query = e.target.value.trim().toLowerCase(); if (query.length === 0) { suggestions.classList.remove('show'); return; } const locations = currentDynasty === 'northern' ? NORTHERN_SONG_LOCATIONS : SOUTHERN_SONG_LOCATIONS; const results = locations.filter(loc => loc.name.includes(query) || loc.modern.includes(query) || loc.herbs.some(h => h.includes(query)) ); if (results.length > 0) { suggestions.innerHTML = results.map(loc => ` <div class="suggestion-item" onclick="selectLocation('${loc.id}')"> <div class="suggestion-icon">\U0001f4cd</div> <div class="suggestion-info"> <div class="suggestion-name">${loc.name}</div> <div class="suggestion-desc">${loc.modern} \u00b7 ${loc.herbs.join('\u3001')}</div> </div> </div> `).join(''); suggestions.classList.add('show'); } else { suggestions.innerHTML = '<div style="padding:12px;color:var(--txt3);text-align:center">未找到相关结果</div>'; suggestions.classList.add('show'); } }); document.addEventListener('click', (e) => { if (!e.target.closest('.search-panel')) { suggestions.classList.remove('show'); } }); } function selectLocation(id) { const locations = currentDynasty === 'northern' ? NORTHERN_SONG_LOCATIONS : SOUTHERN_SONG_LOCATIONS; const loc = locations.find(l => l.id === id); if (loc) { document.getElementById('searchInput').value = loc.name; document.getElementById('searchSuggestions').classList.remove('show'); showLocationInfo(loc, 'ancient'); } } // ========== UTILITIES ========== // ========== BASEMAP SWITCHING ========== let currentBasemap = 'whitesmoke'; function switchBasemap(basemap) { currentBasemap = basemap; // 更新按钮样式 document.querySelectorAll('.basemap-btn').forEach(btn => { const isActive = btn.dataset.basemap === basemap; btn.classList.toggle('active', isActive); btn.style.background = isActive ? 'rgba(0,212,170,0.1)' : 'transparent'; btn.style.borderColor = isActive ? 'var(--cyan)' : 'transparent'; btn.style.color = isActive ? 'var(--cyan)' : 'var(--txt2)'; }); // 切换地图样式 if (map) { switch(basemap) { case 'satellite': map.setMapStyle('amap://styles/satellite'); if (mapLeft) mapLeft.setMapStyle('amap://styles/satellite'); showToast('已切换至卫星图', 'success'); break; case 'normal': map.setMapStyle('amap://styles/normal'); if (mapLeft) mapLeft.setMapStyle('amap://styles/normal'); showToast('已切换至道路图', 'success'); break; case 'whitesmoke': default: map.setMapStyle('amap://styles/whitesmoke'); if (mapLeft) mapLeft.setMapStyle('amap://styles/whitesmoke'); showToast('已切换至水墨风格', 'success'); break; } } } // ========== FULLSCREEN MODE ========== let isFullscreen = false; function toggleFullscreen() { if (!document.fullscreenElement) { document.documentElement.requestFullscreen().then(() => { isFullscreen = true; document.body.classList.add('fullscreen'); showToast('已进入全屏模式，按ESC退出', 'success'); }).catch(err => { console.error('全屏模式失败:', err); }); } else { document.exitFullscreen().then(() => { isFullscreen = false; document.body.classList.remove('fullscreen'); }); } } // 监听全屏变化 document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', () => { if (!document.fullscreenElement) { isFullscreen = false; document.body.classList.remove('fullscreen'); } }); // ========== HEATMAP ========== function toggleHeatmap(show) { if (!map) return; if (show) { // 加载热力图数据 const locations = currentDynasty === 'northern' ? NORTHERN_SONG_LOCATIONS : SOUTHERN_SONG_LOCATIONS; // 构建热力图数据点 const heatmapData = locations.map(loc => ({ lng: loc.pos[0], lat: loc.pos[1], count: loc.herbs.length * 10 // 根据药材数量加权 })); // 使用高德热力图插件 if (typeof AMap.HeatMap !== 'undefined') { if (!heatmapLayer) { heatmapLayer = new AMap.HeatMap(map, { radius: 25, opacity: [0, 0.8], gradient: { 0.5: 'blue', 0.65: 'rgb(117,211,248)', 0.7: 'rgb(0, 255, 0)', 0.9: '#ffea00', 1.0: 'red' } }); } heatmapLayer.setDataSet({ data: heatmapData, max: 30 }); heatmapLayer.show(); showToast('热力图已开启', 'success'); } else { // 如果没有热力图插件，使用圆形覆盖物模拟 showSimpleHeatmap(heatmapData); } } else { if (heatmapLayer) { heatmapLayer.hide(); } hideSimpleHeatmap(); } } // 简化版热力图（使用圆形覆盖物） let simpleHeatmapCircles = []; function showSimpleHeatmap(data) { hideSimpleHeatmap(); data.forEach(point => { const circle = new AMap.Circle({ center: [point.lng, point.lat], radius: 50000 + point.count * 5000, // 半径根据权重调整 fillColor: '#ff4d4f', fillOpacity: 0.3, strokeColor: '#ff7875', strokeWeight: 1, strokeOpacity: 0.5 }); map.add(circle); simpleHeatmapCircles.push(circle); }); } function hideSimpleHeatmap() { simpleHeatmapCircles.forEach(circle => map.remove(circle)); simpleHeatmapCircles = []; } // ========== DRUG-SPECIFIC HEATMAP ========== function showDrugHeatmap(drugName) { if (!map) return; // 查找该药物的所有产地 const locations = currentDynasty === 'northern' ? NORTHERN_SONG_LOCATIONS : SOUTHERN_SONG_LOCATIONS; const drugLocations = locations.filter(loc => loc.herbs.some(h => h.includes(drugName)) ); if (drugLocations.length === 0) { showToast(`未找到${drugName}的产地数据`, 'error'); return; } // 构建热力图数据 const heatmapData = drugLocations.map(loc => ({ lng: loc.pos[0], lat: loc.pos[1], count: 20 })); // 高亮显示这些产地 drugLocations.forEach(loc => { const marker = new AMap.CircleMarker({ center: loc.pos, radius: 15, fillColor: '#ff4d4f', fillOpacity: 0.8, strokeColor: '#fff', strokeWeight: 2 }); map.add(marker); // 3秒后移除高亮 setTimeout(() => map.remove(marker), 3000); }); // 自适应视野 map.setFitView(); showToast(`${drugName}产地分布已高亮`, 'success'); } function resetView() { if (!map) { showToast('地图尚未加载完成', 'error'); return; } map.setZoomAndCenter(5, [112.0, 35.0]); closeInfoPanel(); showToast('视图已重置', 'success'); } function showToast(message, type = 'info') { const container = document.getElementById('toastContainer'); const toast = document.createElement('div'); toast.className = `toast ${type}`; toast.textContent = message; container.appendChild(toast); setTimeout(() => { toast.remove(); }, 3000); } // ========== User Menu ========== function initUserMenu() { const isLoggedIn = localStorage.getItem('isLoggedIn'); const loginBtn = document.getElementById('loginBtn'); const userAvatar = document.getElementById('userAvatar'); const navUserAvatar = document.getElementById('navUserAvatar'); if (isLoggedIn === 'true') { const user = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('benCaoUser') || '{}'); if (loginBtn) loginBtn.style.display = 'none'; if (userAvatar) { userAvatar.style.display = 'flex'; if (navUserAvatar) navUserAvatar.src = user.avatar || ''; } } else { if (loginBtn) loginBtn.style.display = 'block'; if (userAvatar) userAvatar.style.display = 'none'; } } function goToProfile() { window.location.href = 'profile.html'; } function goToLogin() { window.location.href = 'login.html'; } function toggleUserDropdown() { const dd = document.getElementById('userDropdown'); if (dd) dd.classList.toggle('show'); } function logout() { localStorage.removeItem('isLoggedIn'); localStorage.removeItem('benCaoUser'); window.location.reload(); } // 点击外部关闭下拉 window.addEventListener('click', function(e) { const avatar = document.getElementById('userAvatar'); const dd = document.getElementById('userDropdown'); if (dd && avatar && !avatar.contains(e.target) && !dd.contains(e.target)) { dd.classList.remove('show'); } }); // 页面加载时初始化用户菜单 initUserMenu(); </script>
